## 2022-11-29

> 1/ Based on some of the folks who have reached out to me recently, I get the sense there's appetite for news stories and analysis around the field of AI safety. If you're a reporter, all I ask is this: focus on the technical stuff, not the personalities or the drama.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1597529496529252352 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20251202050939/https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1597529496529252352)

## 2022-11-12 · reply to @jachiam0

> Okay, one last thing: for the love of all that is holy please take the FAccT crowd more seriously on social issues, given how much of the AI risk landscape is shaped by "an AI persuades a human to feel or believe something"

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591497091804987393 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20221113003932/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1591497091804987393)

## 2022-11-12

> 🧵 to clarify my views on AGI, timelines, and x-risk. TL;DR: My AGI timelines are short-ish (within two decades with things getting weird soon) and I think x-risk is real, but I think probabilities of doom by AGI instrumentally opting to kill us are greatly exaggerated. 1/

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591494093766787076 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20221113003429/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1591494093766787076)

## 2022-11-12 · reply to @JMannhart

> @JMannhart Ah - my claims are
> * P(everyone dead in 10 years) is extremely small (1e-6),
> * P(everyone dead in 100 years) is much less than 100%,
> * Most discourse around x-risk neglects to consider or characterize gradual transformations of humanity that strike me as moderately plausible

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591391800346152960 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20251202043611/https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591391800346152960)

## 2022-11-12 · reply to @jachiam0

> @adamrisom @mimi10v3 Again, this is not a claim that x-risk isn't real, that AGI doesn't lead to x-risk, or that AGI doesn't have potentially catastrophic impacts, all of which I think are plausible claims. But the claimed timelines and probabilities are just way, way out of connection to reality.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591309590473633792 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20251202044813/https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591309590473633792)

## 2022-11-12 · reply to @LeetAlpaca3

> @LeetAlpaca3 P(Misaligned AGI doom by 2032): &lt;1e-6%

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591221752566542336 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20240927224211/https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591221752566542336)

## 2022-11-12 · reply to @jachiam0

> (This is not to say there are no x-risks from AGI - I think there are - but anyone who tells you probabilities are in the 5-10% range or greater that AGI will immediately and intentionally kill everyone is absolutely not thinking clearly)

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591220715872976897 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20230715042240/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1591220715872976897)

## 2022-11-12 · reply to @jachiam0

> It seems like a huge miss to me that an enormous amount of ink gets spilled over speculative AGI tail risks predicted by people who are clearly converting anxiety disorders into made-up numbers about the likelihood of everyone dying within 10 years...

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591220710122590209 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20240927220817/https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591220710122590209)

## 2022-11-12

> Three big pieces of practical advice for EA right now: 1) stop doing groupthink that EA is intrinsically more-trustworthy than everyone else / better at everything than everyone else, because this is a hazardous belief that leads to excessive risk-taking and correlated risks

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1591220703361404930 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20221112152125/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1591220703361404930)

## 2022-11-11

> If you're an EA, even if SBF did not commit any fraud and this was just a black swan risk event, you should still be freaking out because this would be an extremely high profile example of "EA cannot manage tail risks despite longtermism revolving around managing tail risks"

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1590886677245235200 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20221111211038/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1590886677245235200)

## 2022-09-10 · reply to @jachiam0

> On the other hand, even though this release was open source and therefore not motivated by profit, the large dataset of art used to train it is unquestionably exploitative of the artists who made it since they did not consent to it and cannot capture downstream value.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1568610123706245121 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20221124203139/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1568610123706245121)

## 2022-09-10

> I feel very conflicted about the Stable Diffusion open source release.

## 2022-09-04

> Are there any fantasy stories that have the flavor of early computer programmers doing arcane debugging?

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1566381809566302209 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220904142612/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1566381809566302209)

## 2022-09-03

> I strongly prefer due process and minimal paternalism from companies when it comes to acting in the capacity of an unelected government for regulating free speech. But Kiwifarms 1000% had this coming. You can't gleefully host hate/doxxing campaigns to drive people to suicide. No.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1566204437290373120 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220904062036/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1566204437290373120)

## 2022-09-03 · reply to @jachiam0

> He was also principled and thoughtful in his approach to  mitigating AI risks. He was an excellent colleague. The field has lost someone who had much to contribute.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1565944418216996864 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220903082430/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1565944418216996864)

## 2022-09-03 · reply to @jachiam0

> He was warm, compassionate, and sincerely supportive to me in ways that helped me get through it. We would go for walks around the Mission and have long conversations about life, the universe, and everything. He was always insightful.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1565944415272587264 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220903060756/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1565944415272587264)

## 2022-09-01

> Who would be up to give feedback on an ~800 word blog post draft about the "missing middle" in AGI discussions? A piece about AGI conceptions that are neither maximalist nor denialist.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1565240243669721088 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220901072917/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1565240243669721088)

## 2022-09-01

> "foom 2024" AGI maximalists are also ridiculous, to be clear.

Quoting https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1565067146140323841:
> Honestly kind of baffled about the skepticism that AGI ever can or will exist, given the strides in AI over the past few decades have been pretty relentless. Sure, maybe it's not happening in six months, but ten years? You really want to bet hard on "no"? https://t.co/mJs7GCYb4y

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1565176832885813248 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220901054217/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1565176832885813248)

## 2022-08-31 · reply to @sahiralsaid

> @sahiralsaid Agree to disagree on whether the gap to AGI is a difference in kind vs a difference in scale, tweaks, and more-or-less existing architectural elements getting combined.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1565093066754519040 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220901083848/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1565093066754519040)

## 2022-08-17

> There's something wonderful about finally figuring out what direction to take the plot of a half-finished novel that you've been slowly working on for years.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1559707874326200321 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220817010616/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1559707874326200321)

## 2022-08-12

> More broadly: do you see it as your imperative to change the world in ways it may not consent to be changed, if you think the utility calculus is exceptionally good?

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1558235828769497089 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220813134348/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1558235828769497089)

## 2022-08-09

> hot take: no clear distinction between alignment work and capabilities work yet. might not be for a year or two.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1557105500679970817 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220810030154/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1557105500679970817)

## 2022-08-09

> A warm, sincere reminder to all that you don't need permission, or to be perfect, to be worthy of love, respect, compassion, or the internal experience of joy. I hope you are having a good night, dear stranger.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1556831984667828224 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220809023934/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1556831984667828224)

## 2022-08-07

> Some rules I set for myself in this one: 1) If there's a sentence, make it longer. 2) If there's an error, leave it; if there isn't, add one. 3) Use as many semicolons as possible. 4) Utilize the thesaurus copiously. 5) Wherever the wrong word can be deplored, deplore it.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1556143691156398080 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220807050420/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1556143691156398080)

## 2022-08-02 · reply to @acapellascience

> @acapellascience How did I rhyme "minor"? lol, quite poorly, it does not stand up to scrutiny

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1554586927139115010 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220802215755/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1554586927139115010)

## 2022-08-02 · reply to @jachiam0

> @acapellascience Yes there were times / when I broke through
> And thought I'd done / something brand new
> But then I searched / for it online
> There's a paper / from '89
> Erase it all / I've hit a wall
> My result's minor

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1554584091873386500 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220802214704/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1554584091873386500)

## 2022-08-02 · reply to @acapellascience

> @acapellascience Once, while despairing an impending conference deadline, I wrote a "My Way" parody called "My Result's Minor." Probably didn't scan well and the content wouldn't be very relatable or informative, but it was fun!

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1554583728646660097 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220802214458/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1554583728646660097)

## 2022-08-02

> What's the most ridiculous extended bit you've ever seen anyone commit to? Something that required energy input *way* in excess of the comedic return on investment.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1554334411109404672 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20220802053139/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1554334411109404672)

## 2022-07-21

> I wish we had better social mechanisms to evaluate whole people - all the good and all the bad, not just the parts we see for ourselves but all of it.

> The humans have the obligation to mitigate and prevent harm. The humans are the ones making the choices and trade-offs about risk. The humans are the ones deciding who gets represented in decisions around how the AI is built and used. The humans are morally responsible.

> 
> A quick summary (heh):

> We want our AI systems to be aligned with human intentions.

https://x.com/jachiam0/status/1410537839385669632 · [archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20210701101124/https://twitter.com/jachiam0/status/1410537839385669632)

> RL twitter: does anyone have a quick reference for a proof that lim gamma--&gt;1 (1-gamma) * E [sum_{t=0}^{inf} gamma^t r_t] = lim T --&gt; inf (1/T) E[sum_{t=0}^T r_t] ? I'm moderately sure this is true but can't prove off the cuff.
